# Government drafts, 2026-10-01: letters to local government offices

Review copy only: nothing is sent. Kiswahili first, with an English meaning for review.

## The angle

Silverleaf is not selling to these offices and asks them for nothing for themselves. The request is **access to a forum parents already trust**: a 15 to 20 minute free talk in Kiswahili at a community meeting on getting children ready for school, learning at home and the admissions calendar, with a take-home booklet. Parents who want to hear more fill in Silverleaf's own form, and only if they choose to.

- **Order:** a protocol introduction to the council first (it advises and introduces us to the ward executive officers), then the convening request to each ward or village, only after the council has introduced us. District and regional offices get a courtesy notice only.
- **Message:** what the community gets (useful, free, in their language, no obligation). The letter states two safeguards plainly: no resident lists or residents' information, and no payments or gifts to anyone. It states no service, fee or offer: the parent booklet is mentioned, not described. Beyond the letter, the rules are no party or campaign involvement and no criticism of public schools.
- **Ask:** a short meeting, in person or by phone, to discuss the details (the wards also ask for the 15 to 20 minute slot at their next community meeting).
- **Hook:** none that is personal. The honest, sourced reasons are the office's role in convening the meeting and, for a ward or village, how far it is from our nearest campus. We do not flatter an official, use a personal fact about them, or claim government endorsement.
- **What is deliberately left out:** fees, discounts and the list of services (they read as too salesy; they are in the booklet and the replies), the staff discount (it could read as an inducement to officials whose cooperation we seek), sponsorship, and any ask of the official personally. The letter goes to the office by title, not to a named person.
- **Language and form:** formal Kiswahili on letterhead, delivered by hand or to the official postal address, closing "Wako katika ujenzi wa Taifa". The Kiswahili needs a native-speaker review before use.

**Counts.** 9 councils (4 pilot councils are ready: Arusha City Council, Arusha District Council, Hai District Council, Meru District Council); 10 district and regional offices (held); 130 ward and village offices (held until the council has introduced us). Wards are listed nearest first; 31 are more than 20 km from the nearest campus and belong in a later wave.

---

# 1. Councils: protocol introduction (9)

### 1.1 Halmashauri ya Jiji la Arusha (Arusha City Council)

- To: Mkurugenzi wa Halmashauri (Council Director) | Delivery: Official office address (hand delivery) | Plan: M9dd148842379 | Status: draft_ready

**Kiswahili.** YAH: OMBI LA UTAMBULISHO ILI KUTOA ELIMU KWA WAZAZI KUHUSU MAANDALIZI YA WATOTO KUANZA SHULE

```
Mkurugenzi wa Jiji,
Halmashauri ya Jiji la Arusha,
S.L.P. 3013,
ARUSHA.

YAH: OMBI LA UTAMBULISHO ILI KUTOA ELIMU KWA WAZAZI KUHUSU MAANDALIZI YA WATOTO KUANZA SHULE

Silverleaf Academy ni shule binafsi inayofundisha kwa lugha ya Kiingereza, yenye kampasi tano: Arusha City (Sakina), Kijenge na Ilboru jijini Arusha, Usa River, na Boma Ng'ombe.

Tunaomba ushauri wako na barua ya utambulisho kwa Watendaji wa Kata ili, pale itakapofaa, tupewe muda mfupi wa dakika 15 hadi 20 katika mikutano ya wananchi. Tutatoa elimu bure kwa Kiswahili kuhusu maandalizi ya mtoto kuanza shule, kujifunza nyumbani na ratiba ya udahili. Kila mzazi atapata kijitabu cha mzazi.

Tungependa kuanzia katika kata zilizo karibu na kampasi zetu, kama vile Osunyai Jr, Sokon I na Sombetini.

Mzazi atakayependa taarifa zaidi atajaza fomu yetu kwa hiari yake. Hatutaomba orodha ya wakazi wala taarifa zozote za wakazi, na hatutatoa malipo wala zawadi kwa mtu yeyote.

Je, itawezekana kupanga kikao kifupi, ana kwa ana au kwa simu, ili kujadili maelezo?

Wako katika ujenzi wa Taifa,

Mariam Haji
Mratibu wa Masoko na Ushirikiano, Silverleaf Academy
S.L.P. 14146, Usa River · +255 769 486 660 · info@silverleaf.co.tz
```

**English meaning, for review:**

```
To the City Director, Arusha City Council, P.O. Box 3013, Arusha.

RE: REQUEST FOR AN INTRODUCTION TO GIVE PARENTS INFORMATION ON PREPARING CHILDREN TO START SCHOOL

Silverleaf Academy is a private English-medium school with five campuses: Arusha City (Sakina), Kijenge and Ilboru in Arusha, Usa River, and Boma Ng'ombe.

We ask for your advice and a letter introducing us to the ward executive officers so that, where appropriate, we can have a short 15 to 20 minutes at community meetings. We will give a free talk in Kiswahili on getting children ready for school, learning at home and the admissions calendar. Every parent will receive a parent booklet.

We would like to start in the wards nearest our campuses, such as Osunyai Jr, Sokon I and Sombetini.

Parents who want more information will fill in our form only if they choose to. We will not ask for resident lists or any residents' information, and we will not offer payments or gifts to anyone.

Would it be possible to arrange a short meeting, either in person or by phone, to discuss the details?

Yours in nation building,

Mariam Haji
Marketing and Partnership Coordinator, Silverleaf Academy
```

**Follow-up after 5 working days (Kiswahili):**

```
Mkurugenzi wa Jiji,
Halmashauri ya Jiji la Arusha,
S.L.P. 3013,
ARUSHA.

YAH: UFUATILIAJI WA BARUA YETU YA OMBI LA UTAMBULISHO

Tunafuatilia barua yetu kuhusu elimu kwa wazazi kuhusu maandalizi ya watoto kuanza shule. Je, itawezekana kupanga kikao kifupi, ana kwa ana au kwa simu, ili kujadili maelezo?

Wako katika ujenzi wa Taifa,

Mariam Haji
Mratibu wa Masoko na Ushirikiano, Silverleaf Academy
S.L.P. 14146, Usa River · +255 769 486 660 · info@silverleaf.co.tz
```

```
S.L.P. 3003,
MOSHI.

YAH: OMBI LA UTAMBULISHO ILI KUTOA ELIMU KWA WAZAZI KUHUSU MAANDALIZI YA WATOTO KUANZA SHULE

Silverleaf Academy ni shule binafsi inayofundisha kwa lugha ya Kiingereza, yenye kampasi tano: Arusha City (Sakina), Kijenge na Ilboru jijini Arusha, Usa River, na Boma Ng'ombe.

Tunaomba ushauri wako na barua ya utambulisho kwa Watendaji wa Kata ili, pale itakapofaa, tupewe muda mfupi wa dakika 15 hadi 20 katika mikutano ya wananchi. Tutatoa elimu bure kwa Kiswahili kuhusu maandalizi ya mtoto kuanza shule, kujifunza nyumbani na ratiba ya udahili. Kila mzazi atapata kijitabu cha mzazi.

Tungependa kuanzia katika kata zilizo karibu na kampasi zetu, kama vile Kindi, Uru Kusini na Kibosho Magharibi.

Mzazi atakayependa taarifa zaidi atajaza fomu yetu kwa hiari yake. Hatutaomba orodha ya wakazi wala taarifa zozote za wakazi, na hatutatoa malipo wala zawadi kwa mtu yeyote.

Je, itawezekana kupanga kikao kifupi, ana kwa ana au kwa simu, ili kujadili maelezo?

Wako katika ujenzi wa Taifa,

Mariam Haji
Mratibu wa Masoko na Ushirikiano, Silverleaf Academy
S.L.P. 14146, Usa River · +255 769 486 660 · info@silverleaf.co.tz
```

**English meaning, for review:**

```
To the District Executive Director, Moshi District Council, P.O. Box 3003, Moshi.

RE: REQUEST FOR AN INTRODUCTION TO GIVE PARENTS INFORMATION ON PREPARING CHILDREN TO START SCHOOL

Silverleaf Academy is a private English-medium school with five campuses: Arusha City (Sakina), Kijenge and Ilboru in Arusha, Usa River, and Boma Ng'ombe.

We ask for your advice and a letter introducing us to the ward executive officers so that, where appropriate, we can have a short 15 to 20 minutes at community meetings. We will give a free talk in Kiswahili on getting children ready for school, learning at home and the admissions calendar. Every parent will receive a parent booklet.

We would like to start in the wards nearest our campuses, such as Kindi, Uru Kusini and Kibosho Magharibi.

Parents who want more information will fill in our form only if they choose to. We will not ask for resident lists or any residents' information, and we will not offer payments or gifts to anyone.

Would it be possible to arrange a short meeting, either in person or by phone, to discuss the details?

Yours in nation building,

Mariam Haji
Marketing and Partnership Coordinator, Silverleaf Academy
```

```
Kwa utambulisho wa Halmashauri husika, tunapanga kutoa elimu bure kwa Kiswahili kuhusu maandalizi ya mtoto kuanza shule, kujifunza nyumbani na ratiba ya udahili katika mikutano ya wananchi, kwa muda mfupi utakaotolewa na Watendaji wa Kata. # 3. Wards and villages: convening request (130)
```

## Arusha City Council (pilot council)

### 3.1 Ofisi ya Kijiji cha Mwanama (Arusha City Council)

- To: Afisa Mtendaji wa Kijiji (Village Executive Officer (VEO)) | Delivery: Official office address (hand delivery) | Plan: Mf7b587077312 | Status: needs_review
- Nearest campus: Kijenge, 0.4 km (first wave (up to 10 km))
- **Held:** Held until the council introduction (GA01) is recorded and this post is verified within 90 days (GA02 gate).

**Kiswahili.** YAH: OMBI LA NAFASI FUPI KATIKA MKUTANO MKUU WA KIJIJI CHA MWANAMA

```
Afisa Mtendaji wa Kijiji cha Mwanama,
Halmashauri ya Jiji la Arusha.

YAH: OMBI LA NAFASI FUPI KATIKA MKUTANO MKUU WA KIJIJI CHA MWANAMA

Kufuatia utambulisho wa Halmashauri, Silverleaf Academy inaomba nafasi ya dakika 15 hadi 20 katika mkutano mkuu ujao wa Kijiji cha Mwanama, ili kutoa elimu bure kwa Kiswahili kuhusu maandalizi ya mtoto kuanza shule, kujifunza nyumbani na ratiba ya udahili.

Kampasi yetu iliyo karibu zaidi ni Kijenge, chini ya kilomita 1 kutoka kijiji chenu.

Kila mzazi atapata kijitabu cha mzazi.

Mzazi atakayependa taarifa zaidi atajaza fomu yetu kwa hiari yake. Hatutaomba orodha ya wakazi wala taarifa zozote za wakazi, na hatutatoa malipo wala zawadi kwa mtu yeyote.

Je, itawezekana kupanga kikao kifupi, ana kwa ana au kwa simu, ili kujadili maelezo?

Wako katika ujenzi wa Taifa,

Mariam Haji
Mratibu wa Masoko na Ushirikiano, Silverleaf Academy
S.L.P. 14146, Usa River · +255 769 486 660 · info@silverleaf.co.tz
```

**English meaning, for review:**

```
To the Village Executive Officer, Mwanama, Arusha City Council.

RE: REQUEST FOR A SHORT SLOT AT THE NEXT VILLAGE ASSEMBLY OF MWANAMA

Following the council's introduction, Silverleaf Academy asks for 15 to 20 minutes at the next village assembly of Mwanama, to give a free talk in Kiswahili on getting children ready for school, learning at home and the admissions calendar.

Our nearest campus is Kijenge, less than 1 km from your village.

Every parent will receive a parent booklet.

Parents who want more information will fill in our form only if they choose to. We will not ask for resident lists or any residents' information, and we will not offer payments or gifts to anyone.

Would it be possible to arrange a short meeting, either in person or by phone, to discuss the details?

Yours in nation building,

Mariam Haji
Marketing and Partnership Coordinator, Silverleaf Academy
```

```
Afisa Mtendaji wa Kata ya Majengo,
```
```
Halmashauri ya Manispaa ya Moshi.

YAH: OMBI LA NAFASI FUPI KATIKA MKUTANO WA WANANCHI WA KATA YA MAJENGO

Kufuatia utambulisho wa Halmashauri, Silverleaf Academy inaomba nafasi ya dakika 15 hadi 20 katika mkutano ujao wa wananchi wa Kata ya Majengo, ili kutoa elimu bure kwa Kiswahili kuhusu maandalizi ya mtoto kuanza shule, kujifunza nyumbani na ratiba ya udahili.

Kampasi yetu iliyo karibu zaidi ni Boma Ng'ombe (takriban kilomita 22 kutoka kata yenu).

Kila mzazi atapata kijitabu cha mzazi.

Mzazi atakayependa taarifa zaidi atajaza fomu yetu kwa hiari yake. Hatutaomba orodha ya wakazi wala taarifa zozote za wakazi, na hatutatoa malipo wala zawadi kwa mtu yeyote.

Wako katika ujenzi wa Taifa,

Mariam Haji
Mratibu wa Masoko na Ushirikiano, Silverleaf Academy
S.L.P. 14146, Usa River · +255 769 486 660 · info@silverleaf.co.tz
```

**English meaning, for review:**

```
To the Ward Executive Officer, Majengo, Moshi Municipal Council.

RE: REQUEST FOR A SHORT SLOT AT THE NEXT COMMUNITY MEETING OF MAJENGO WARD

Following the council's introduction, Silverleaf Academy asks for 15 to 20 minutes at the next community meeting of Majengo ward, to give a free talk in Kiswahili on getting children ready for school, learning at home and the admissions calendar.

Our nearest campus is Boma Ng'ombe, about 22 km from your ward.

Every parent will receive a parent booklet.

Parents who want more information will fill in our form only if they choose to. We will not ask for resident lists or any residents' information, and we will not offer payments or gifts to anyone.

Would it be possible to arrange a short meeting, either in person or by phone, to discuss the details?

Yours in nation building,

Mariam Haji
Marketing and Partnership Coordinator, Silverleaf Academy
```
